<div id="evPreloadOverlay" role="status" aria-live="polite">
  <div class="evpl-card">
    <div class="evpl-title" id="evPreloadTitle">Loading project media…</div>
    <div class="evpl-sub" id="evPreloadSub">Preparing playback</div>
    <div class="evpl-bar"><i id="evPreloadBar"></i></div>
    <button type="button" class="evpl-skip" id="evPreloadSkip">Skip</button>
  </div>
</div>
<div id="evBufferPill" role="status" aria-live="polite"><div class="evpl-spin"></div><span id="evBufferText">Buffering…</span></div>

<script id="ethvelle-v199-preload-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__ethvellePreloadV199)return;
  window.__ethvellePreloadV199=true;

  const PLAY_PATH='<path d="M8 5v14l11-7z"/>',PAUSE_PATH='<path d="M6 5h4v14H6zM14 5h4v14h-4z"/>';
  const LOOK_MIN=1,LOOK_MAX=3;
  const num=(v,d=0)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):d;
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const sched=()=>window.__ethvelleAudioSchedulerDiagnostics||null;
  const budget=()=>window.__evDecoderBudget||{pool:3,warm:2};
  if(window.__evRebuffer===undefined)window.__evRebuffer=true;

  const isMedia=ov=>!!ov&&(ov.type==='video'||ov.type==='audio')&&!!ov.src&&!ov.reversePlayback&&num(ov.trimEnd)>num(ov.trimStart);
  const editorActive=()=>{const s=$('screen-editor');return !!s&&s.classList.contains('active');};

  // ---- 1-3s adaptive lookahead: the more cuts coming up, the further ahead we prepare ----
  function lookaheadFor(t){
    let edges=0;
    if(Array.isArray(overlays)){
      for(let i=0;i<overlays.length;i++){
        const ov=overlays[i];if(!isMedia(ov))continue;
        const s=num(ov.trimStart),e=num(ov.trimEnd);
        if(s>t&&s<=t+LOOK_MAX)edges++;
        if(e>t&&e<=t+LOOK_MAX)edges++;
      }
    }
    const w=Math.max(LOOK_MIN,Math.min(LOOK_MAX,LOOK_MIN+edges*.4));
    window.__evPreloadWindowSec=w;
    return w;
  }

  // A split whose second half continues straight on from the first half reuses the same decoder
  // (see transferContinuousSplitVideos), so it needs no separate warm-up.
  function continuesDecoder(prev,next){
    if(!prev||!next||prev.type!=='video'||next.type!=='video'||String(prev.src||'')!==String(next.src||''))return false;
    const ps=Math.max(.05,num(prev.speed,1)),ns=Math.max(.05,num(next.speed,1));
    const srcEnd=num(prev.sourceOffset)+(num(prev.trimEnd)-num(prev.trimStart))*ps;
    return Math.abs(num(prev.trimEnd)-num(next.trimStart))<=.025&&Math.abs(ps-ns)<=.001&&Math.abs(srcEnd-num(next.sourceOffset))<=.06;
  }

  // ---- readiness of everything needed for the next `win` seconds starting at t ----
  function readiness(t,relaxed,activeOnly){
    const win=lookaheadFor(t),t1=t+win;
    const out={win,total:0,done:0,pending:[]};
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return out;
    const d=sched();
    const relevant=overlays.filter(ov=>isMedia(ov)&&num(ov.trimEnd)>t&&num(ov.trimStart)<t1);
    // (1) every source in the window must be decoded to PCM, otherwise the cut would be silent
    const seen=new Set();
    if(!activeOnly)relevant.forEach(ov=>{
      const key=String(ov.src);if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);
      out.total++;
      let st=d?d.cache.get(key):null;
      if(!st&&d){try{d.prime();}catch(_e){}st=d.cache.get(key);}
      if(!st||st.status==='loading')out.pending.push('audio');else out.done++;
    });
    // (2) clips active at t need a presentable frame; (3) the next clips need a pre-seeked decoder
    const vids=relevant.filter(ov=>ov.type==='video');
    const active=vids.filter(ov=>num(ov.trimStart)<=t+1e-4&&num(ov.trimEnd)>t);
    active.forEach(ov=>{
      out.total++;
      const el=overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)];
      const ok=el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&(evOverlayVideoFrameIsPresentable(ov,el,t)||(relaxed&&el.readyState>=2&&!el.seeking));
      if(ok)out.done++;else out.pending.push('frame');
    });
    if(activeOnly)return out;
    const b=budget();
    const slots=Math.max(0,Math.min(b.warm,b.pool-active.length));
    const upcoming=vids.filter(ov=>num(ov.trimStart)>t+1e-4).sort((x,y)=>num(x.trimStart)-num(y.trimStart));
    const need=[];
    for(const ov of upcoming){
      if(need.length>=slots)break;
      if(active.some(a=>continuesDecoder(a,ov))||need.some(a=>continuesDecoder(a,ov)))continue;
      if(overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)])continue;
      need.push(ov);
    }
    // Warm decoders are best-effort: once we've waited a while, stop holding playback for them.
    if(!relaxed)need.forEach(ov=>{
      out.total++;
      const el=overlayWarmEls[String(ov.id)];
      const target=Math.max(0,num(ov.sourceOffset));
      const ok=el&&(el.dataset.evWarmReady==='1'||(el.readyState>=3&&!el.seeking&&Math.abs(num(el.currentTime)-target)<=.08)||
        (relaxed&&el.readyState>=2&&!el.seeking));
      if(ok)out.done++;else out.pending.push('decoder');
    });
    return out;
  }

  // ---- Decoder kick ----
  // Some browsers leave a paused, not-visible <video> stuck in "seeking" (readyState 1) until it is
  // asked to play once; the player code then waits for readyState before playing -> frozen frame.
  // A short muted play() wakes the decoder; afterwards the element is put back exactly where it was.
  function adopted(el){const m=overlayMediaEls;for(const k in m){if(m[k]===el)return true;}return false;}
  function kick(el,target,keepPlaying){
    if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||window.__evNoKick)return false;
    const now=performance.now();
    if(el.__evKicking||now-num(el.__evKickAt)<700)return false;
    el.__evKicking=true;el.__evKickAt=now;
    try{if(window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics)window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics.recordKick();}catch(_e){}
    const done=()=>{
      if(!el.__evKicking)return;el.__evKicking=false;
      try{
        if(adopted(el)&&isPlaying)return;
        el.pause();
        if(Number.isFinite(target)&&Math.abs(num(el.currentTime)-target)>.03)el.currentTime=Math.max(0,target);
      }catch(_e){}
    };
    el.addEventListener('seeked',()=>setTimeout(done,40),{once:true});
    setTimeout(done,1600);
    try{el.muted=true;const p=el.play();if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(()=>{});}catch(_e){el.__evKicking=false;return false;}
    return true;
  }
  // "stuck" = has been seeking / without data for `ms` continuously (a normal seek finishes far sooner)
  function stuckFor(el,ms){
    if(!el)return false;
    const now=performance.now();
    if(!(el.seeking||el.readyState<2)){el.__evStuckSince=0;return false;}
    if(!el.__evStuckSince)el.__evStuckSince=now;
    return now-el.__evStuckSince>=ms;
  }
  function kickActive(t,keepPlaying){
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    for(let i=0;i<overlays.length;i++){
      const ov=overlays[i];
      if(!ov||ov.type!=='video'||ov.reversePlayback||!ov.src)continue;
      if(!(t>=num(ov.trimStart)&&t<num(ov.trimEnd)))continue;
      const el=overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)];
      if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO'||!stuckFor(el,keepPlaying?350:900))continue;
      let ex=NaN;try{ex=evOverlayVideoExpectedLocalTime(ov,t,el);}catch(_e){}
      kick(el,ex,keepPlaying);
    }
  }
  function kickWarm(sinceMs){
    for(const k in overlayWarmEls){
      const el=overlayWarmEls[k];
      if(!el||el.dataset.evWarmReady==='1'||!stuckFor(el,1200))continue;
      kick(el,num(el.dataset.evWarmTarget,NaN),false);
    }
  }

  // ---- polling wait: resolves 'ready' | 'timeout' | 'cancelled' ----
  function waitReady(opts){
    const cap=opts.cap||5000,relaxAfter=opts.relaxAfter||2000,activeOnly=!!opts.activeOnly,onProgress=opts.onProgress;
    let cancelled=false,lastRender=0;const started=performance.now();
    const promise=new Promise(resolve=>{
      const poll=()=>{
        if(cancelled)return resolve('cancelled');
        const now=performance.now(),elapsed=now-started,t=num(currentAbsoluteTimeSec);
        try{warmUpcomingOverlayVideos(t);}catch(_e){}
        // A paused render creates the <video> element of any clip that is active at the playhead.
        // Only done when one is missing: re-rendering a ready paused clip would re-seek it.
        if(now-lastRender>400&&Array.isArray(overlays)&&overlays.some(ov=>ov&&ov.type==='video'&&!ov.reversePlayback&&ov.src&&t>=num(ov.trimStart)&&t<num(ov.trimEnd)&&!overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)])){
          lastRender=now;try{renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
        }
        try{kickActive(t,false);}catch(_e){}
        if(!activeOnly){try{kickWarm();}catch(_e){}}
        let r;try{r=readiness(t,elapsed>relaxAfter,activeOnly);}catch(err){r={win:1,total:0,done:0,pending:[]};}
        if(onProgress){try{onProgress(r,elapsed);}catch(_e){}}
        if(!r.pending.length)return resolve('ready');
        if(elapsed>=cap)return resolve('timeout');
        setTimeout(poll,60);
      };
      poll();
    });
    return {promise,cancel(){cancelled=true;}};
  }

  // ---- UI ----
  function setPlayIcon(playing){const i=$('playIcon');if(i)i.innerHTML=playing?PAUSE_PATH:PLAY_PATH;}
  function showPill(text){
    const pill=$('evBufferPill');if(!pill)return;
    const tx=$('evBufferText');if(tx)tx.textContent=text||'Buffering…';
    const stage=$('canvasStage');
    if(stage){const r=stage.getBoundingClientRect();if(r.width>0){pill.style.left=(r.left+r.width/2)+'px';pill.style.top=(r.top+r.height/2)+'px';}}
    pill.classList.add('show');
  }
  function hidePill(){const p=$('evBufferPill');if(p)p.classList.remove('show');}

  // ---- Play gate (before start and for mid-playback rebuffer) ----
  let pending=null,internalPause=false,playStartedAt=0;
  const stallCount=new Map();
  function cancelPending(restoreIcon){
    if(!pending)return;
    try{pending.cancel();}catch(_e){}
    pending=null;hidePill();
    if(restoreIcon)setPlayIcon(false);
  }
  function runGate(startFn,label,cap,rebuffer,beforeStart){
    const token={};pending=token;
    setPlayIcon(true);showPill(label);
    const w=waitReady({cap,relaxAfter:rebuffer?1400:2600,activeOnly:rebuffer,onProgress:(r)=>{if(pending===token&&r.total&&!rebuffer)showPill(label+' '+Math.min(r.done,r.total)+'/'+r.total);}});
    token.cancel=w.cancel;
    // The media/frame preload and audio startup are separate gates. In particular, a
    // suspended Android WebView AudioContext must finish resume() before the timeline's
    // performance clock starts, otherwise the audio clock begins behind and gets re-anchored.
    Promise.all([w.promise,Promise.resolve(beforeStart).then(v=>v!==false,()=>false)]).then(([res,canStart])=>{
      if(pending!==token)return;
      pending=null;
      if(res==='cancelled'){hidePill();return;}
      if(isPlaying||!editorActive()){hidePill();setPlayIcon(isPlaying);return;}
      if(!canStart){hidePill();setPlayIcon(false);if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Audio is not ready yet. Please tap Play again.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2200});return;}
      if(!rebuffer)hidePill();
      window.__evPreloadBypass=true;
      let started;
      try{started=startFn();}catch(err){window.__evPreloadBypass=false;hidePill();setPlayIcon(isPlaying);if(window.__evVideoDebug)console.warn('[evPreload] playback start:',err);return;}
      finally{window.__evPreloadBypass=false;}
      if(rebuffer&&started&&typeof started.then==='function'){
        started.then(()=>hidePill(),err=>{hidePill();setPlayIcon(isPlaying);if(window.__evVideoDebug)console.warn('[evRebuffer] resume:',err);});
      }else if(rebuffer)hidePill();
    });
  }

  function activeAudioStartup(t){
    const d=sched();
    if(!d||!d.cache||!Array.isArray(overlays))return {pending:false,promise:Promise.resolve(true)};
    // Populate the native-video ID set before building the startup wait-list. Video sound
    // no longer needs a second network fetch/decode; only standalone audio clips gate on
    // the unified AudioBuffer cache.
    try{d.prime();}catch(_e){}
    const nativeVideoIds=window.__ethvelleNativeLongVideoAudioIds;
    const sources=Array.from(new Set(overlays.filter(ov=>ov&&ov.src&&!ov.reversePlayback&&
      (ov.type==='audio'||(ov.type==='video'&&!(nativeVideoIds instanceof Set&&nativeVideoIds.has(String(ov.id)))))&&
      num(ov.trimStart)<=t&&num(ov.trimEnd)>t).map(ov=>String(ov.src))));
    if(!sources.length)return {pending:false,promise:Promise.resolve(true)};
    const entries=sources.map(src=>{
      let entry=d.cache.get(src);
      if(!entry&&typeof d.load==='function'){try{d.load(src);entry=d.cache.get(src);}catch(_e){}}
      return entry;
    });
    const pending=entries.some(entry=>!entry||entry.status==='loading');
    const promise=Promise.all(entries.map(entry=>entry&&entry.promise?entry.promise:Promise.resolve(entry)))
      .then(()=>true,()=>false);
    return {pending,promise};
  }
  function installStartGate(){
    const prev=window.startPlayback;
    if(typeof prev!=='function'||prev.__evV199Gate)return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      if(window.__evPreloadBypass)return prev.apply(this,arguments);
      if(pending){cancelPending(true);return;}           // second tap while preparing = cancel
      if(isPlaying)return prev.apply(this,arguments);
      stallCount.clear();
      let t=num(currentAbsoluteTimeSec),contextReady=Promise.resolve(true),contextPending=false;
      try{
        const ctx=typeof ensureAudioOverlayContext==='function'?ensureAudioOverlayContext():null;
        if(ctx&&ctx.state!=='running'){
          contextPending=true;
          // Invoke resume synchronously inside the user's Play gesture, then wait for its
          // promise before anchoring playback to performance.now().
          contextReady=Promise.resolve(ctx.resume()).then(()=>ctx.state==='running',()=>false);
        }
      }catch(_e){contextReady=Promise.resolve(false);contextPending=true;}
      try{
        const end=getProjectContentEndSec();
        if(end>0&&t>=end-1/Math.max(1,num(FPS,30))){setCurrentAbsoluteTime(0);t=0;try{renderFrame();}catch(_e){}}
      }catch(_e){}
      let r=null;try{r=readiness(t,false);}catch(_e){}
      const audioStart=activeAudioStartup(t);
      const startReady=Promise.all([contextReady,audioStart.promise]).then(values=>values.every(Boolean),()=>false);
      if(!r||r.pending.length||contextPending||audioStart.pending){
        runGate(()=>window.startPlayback(),'Preparing playback…',10000,false,startReady);
        return;
      }
      // Both decoder and audio are already ready; no artificial delay is introduced.
      return prev.apply(this,arguments);
    };
    wrapped.__evV199Gate=true;
    window.startPlayback=wrapped;try{startPlayback=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  function installPauseGuards(){
    ['pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime','pausePlayback'].forEach(name=>{
      const prev=window[name];
      if(typeof prev!=='function'||prev.__evV199Guard)return;
      const wrapped=function(){
        if(!internalPause&&pending)cancelPending(true);
        return prev.apply(this,arguments);
      };
      wrapped.__evV199Guard=true;
      window[name]=wrapped;
      try{if(name==='pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime')pausePlaybackAtCurrentTime=wrapped;else pausePlayback=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
    });
  }

  // ---- Rebuffer safety net: only a continuous decoder stall can pause the clock ----
  function checkRebuffer(now){
    if(!window.__evRebuffer||!isPlaying||internalPause||pending||!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    if(now-playStartedAt<400)return;
    const t=num(currentAbsoluteTimeSec);
    let bad=null;
    for(let i=0;i<overlays.length;i++){
      const ov=overlays[i];
      if(!ov||ov.type!=='video'||ov.reversePlayback||!ov.src)continue;
      const s=num(ov.trimStart),e=num(ov.trimEnd);
      if(!(t>=s&&t<e))continue;
      const el=overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)];
      // Presentability/alignment can be false during ordinary cut warm-up. Rebuffer only
      // when this actual element has remained seeking or below HAVE_CURRENT_DATA for 600ms.
      if(el&&el.tagName==='VIDEO'&&stuckFor(el,600)){bad=ov;break;}
    }
    if(!bad)return;
    const id=String(bad.id);
    if((stallCount.get(id)||0)>=2)return;
    stallCount.set(id,(stallCount.get(id)||0)+1);
    try{if(window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics)window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics.recordRebuffer();}catch(_e){}
    let suspendTask=Promise.resolve();
    internalPause=true;
    try{
      const d=sched();
      if(d&&typeof d.suspendForRebuffer==='function'){
        suspendTask=d.suspendForRebuffer().catch(err=>{if(window.__evVideoDebug)console.warn('[evRebuffer] suspend:',err);});
      }else{
        const exact=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?getCurrentAbsoluteTime():Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
        if(playbackRaf!==null){try{cancelAnimationFrame(playbackRaf);}catch(_e){}playbackRaf=null;}
        setCurrentAbsoluteTime(exact);isPlaying=false;playbackStartedAtMs=0;playbackStartTimeSec=exact;
      }
    }finally{internalPause=false;}
    runGate(async()=>{
      await suspendTask;
      const d=sched();
      if(!d||typeof d.resumeAfterRebuffer!=='function')throw new Error('Audio scheduler rebuffer controls unavailable');
      const resumed=await d.resumeAfterRebuffer();
      playStartedAt=performance.now();
      return resumed;
    },'Buffering…',2200,true);
  }
  let lastHousekeep=0,lastKick=0;
  function installTickHook(){
    const prev=window.playbackTick;
    if(typeof prev!=='function'||prev.__evV199Tick)return;
    const wrapped=function(ts){
      const r=prev.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        const now=performance.now();
        if(!playStartedAt||!isPlaying)playStartedAt=now;
        if(now-lastHousekeep>200){
          lastHousekeep=now;
          lookaheadFor(num(currentAbsoluteTimeSec));
          const d=sched();if(d)d.prime();
        }
        if(isPlaying&&now-playStartedAt>120&&now-lastKick>110){lastKick=now;kickActive(num(currentAbsoluteTimeSec),true);}
        checkRebuffer(now);
      }catch(_e){}
      return r;
    };
    wrapped.__evV199Tick=true;
    window.playbackTick=wrapped;try{playbackTick=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  // reset the "just started" timer whenever playback (re)starts
  function installStartStamp(){
    const prev=window.startPlayback;
    if(typeof prev!=='function'||prev.__evV199Stamp)return;
    const wrapped=function(){const was=isPlaying;const r=prev.apply(this,arguments);if(!was&&isPlaying)playStartedAt=performance.now();return r;};
    wrapped.__evV199Stamp=true;wrapped.__evV199Gate=prev.__evV199Gate;
    window.startPlayback=wrapped;try{startPlayback=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }

  // ---- Project open / refresh: preload the opening window behind a loading card ----
  let openSerial=0;
  function overlayUI(show){const o=$('evPreloadOverlay');if(o)o.classList.toggle('show',!!show);}
  function beginOpenPreload(proj){
    // openEditor() restores the complete overlays array synchronously before this hook runs.
    // Gate only projects that actually contain video/audio media; image-only projects open as before.
    const projectMedia=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.filter(ov=>ov&&ov.src&&['video','audio'].includes(ov.type)&&!ov.reversePlayback):[];
    if(!projectMedia.length)return;
    const serial=++openSerial;
    const bar=$('evPreloadBar'),sub=$('evPreloadSub'),title=$('evPreloadTitle'),skip=$('evPreloadSkip');
    if(title)title.textContent='Loading project media…';
    if(sub)sub.textContent='Preparing video and audio';
    if(bar)bar.style.width='4%';
    if(skip)skip.classList.remove('show');
    overlayUI(true);
    let w=null,done=false,audioDone=0,audioTotal=0,audioCapTimer=0;
    const finish=()=>{
      if(done)return;done=true;
      if(audioCapTimer)clearTimeout(audioCapTimer);
      if(serial===openSerial)overlayUI(false);
      if(skip)skip.onclick=null;
      try{w&&w.cancel();}catch(_e){}
    };
    if(skip){skip.onclick=()=>{finish();};setTimeout(()=>{if(!done&&serial===openSerial&&skip)skip.classList.add('show');},2200);}
    // Let openEditor's stage sizing/render complete first, then prime every audio source in
    // the restored project. Split clips sharing one URL are decoded only once by the scheduler.
    setTimeout(()=>{
      if(done)return;
      if(serial!==openSerial||currentProject!==proj||!editorActive()){finish();return;}
      let audioJobs=[];
      try{
        const d=sched();
        if(d){
          d.prime();
          const nativeIds=new Set(typeof d.getNativeVideoAudioIds==='function'?d.getNativeVideoAudioIds():[]);
          const srcs=Array.from(new Set((Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[])
            .filter(ov=>ov&&ov.src&&['video','audio'].includes(ov.type)&&!ov.reversePlayback&&(ov.type==='audio'||!nativeIds.has(String(ov.id))))
            .map(ov=>String(ov.src||'')).filter(Boolean)));
          audioTotal=srcs.length;
          audioJobs=srcs.map(src=>{
            let task;
            try{task=typeof d.load==='function'?d.load(src):Promise.resolve(null);}catch(_e){task=Promise.resolve(null);}
            return Promise.resolve(task).catch(()=>null).then(()=>{audioDone++;});
          });
        }
      }catch(err){if(window.__evVideoDebug)console.warn('[evPreload] project audio prime:',err);}
      const audioAll=Promise.all(audioJobs).then(()=>true,()=>false);
      // Do not hold project open forever on a broken remote source. The scheduler keeps
      // pending decodes running in the background after this bounded loading-card window.
      const audioLimit=new Promise(resolve=>{audioCapTimer=setTimeout(()=>resolve(false),15000);});
      const audioReady=Promise.race([audioAll,audioLimit]);
      const updateCombined=(r)=>{
        if(serial!==openSerial||done)return;
        const framePct=r&&r.total?Math.round(Math.min(1,r.done/r.total)*40):40;
        const audioPct=audioTotal?Math.round(Math.min(1,audioDone/audioTotal)*60):60;
        if(bar)bar.style.width=Math.max(4,Math.min(99,framePct+audioPct))+'%';
        if(sub){
          const bits=[];
          if(audioTotal)bits.push('Audio '+Math.min(audioDone,audioTotal)+' of '+audioTotal);
          if(r&&r.total)bits.push('Media '+Math.min(r.done,r.total)+' of '+r.total);
          sub.textContent=bits.length?bits.join(' · '):'Preparing playback';
        }
      };
      w=waitReady({cap:10000,relaxAfter:6500,onProgress:updateCombined});
      Promise.all([w.promise,audioReady]).then(([mediaResult,audioResult])=>{
        if(done||serial!==openSerial||currentProject!==proj||!editorActive()){finish();return;}
        if(bar)bar.style.width='100%';
        if(sub)sub.textContent=audioResult?'Project media ready':'Finishing media in background';
        setTimeout(finish,140);
      });
    },200);
  }
  function installOpenHook(){
    const prev=window.openEditor;
    if(typeof prev!=='function'||prev.__evV199Open)return;
    const wrapped=function(proj){
      cancelPending(false);hidePill();
      const r=prev.apply(this,arguments);
      try{beginOpenPreload(proj);}catch(err){overlayUI(false);}
      return r;
    };
    wrapped.__evV199Open=true;
    window.openEditor=wrapped;try{openEditor=wrapped;}catch(_e){}
  }
  // leaving the editor must never leave the card or pill hanging
  const origGoTo=window.goTo;
  if(typeof origGoTo==='function'&&!origGoTo.__evV199){
    const w=function(name){if(name!=='editor'){cancelPending(false);hidePill();openSerial++;overlayUI(false);}return origGoTo.apply(this,arguments);};
    w.__evV199=true;window.goTo=w;try{goTo=w;}catch(_e){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden&&pending){cancelPending(true);}});

  installStartGate();installStartStamp();installPauseGuards();installTickHook();installOpenHook();

  window.__evPreload={readiness,waitReady,lookaheadFor,get pending(){return !!pending;},stallCount};
})();
</script>


<script id="ethvelle-v203-playback-report-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const diag=window.__evPlaybackDiagnostics;
  if(!diag||!diag.enabled)return; // no button, observers or monitor loop without ?evdebug=1
  const STYLE_ID='ev-v203-report-style';
  if(!document.getElementById(STYLE_ID)){
    const style=document.createElement('style');style.id=STYLE_ID;
    style.textContent='#evV203CopyPlaybackReport{position:fixed;right:12px;bottom:14px;z-index:2147483646;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.28);border-radius:8px;background:#152b56;color:#fff;padding:9px 12px;font:600 12px/1.2 Inter,system-ui,sans-serif;box-shadow:0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,.28);cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px}#evV203CopyPlaybackReport:active{transform:translateY(1px)}#evV203CopyPlaybackReport[disabled]{opacity:.7;cursor:default}';
    document.head.appendChild(style);
  }
  const button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.id='evV203CopyPlaybackReport';button.textContent='Copy playback report';button.setAttribute('aria-label','Copy playback report');
  (document.body||document.documentElement).appendChild(button);

  const stats={gap34:0,gap100:0,maxRafGap:0,longTaskCount:0,longTaskMs:0,maxClockDrift:0,clockOffset:null,lastRaf:null,playingSince:null};
  const safe=(fn,fallback)=>{try{return fn();}catch(_e){return fallback;}};
  const fmt=(v,d=3)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v).toFixed(d):'n/a';
  const trunc=(v,n)=>String(v==null?'':v).slice(0,n);
  let observer=null;
  if(typeof PerformanceObserver!=='undefined'){
    try{
      observer=new PerformanceObserver(list=>{
        const entries=list.getEntries();
        for(let i=0;i<entries.length;i++){
          const e=entries[i];
          const playing=safe(()=>{const d=window.__ethvelleAudioSchedulerDiagnostics;return !!(d&&d.getPlaybackState().playing);},false);
          if(playing){stats.longTaskCount++;stats.longTaskMs+=Number(e.duration)||0;}
        }
      });
      observer.observe({type:'longtask',buffered:true});
    }catch(_e){observer=null;}
  }
  function playbackState(){
    const d=window.__ethvelleAudioSchedulerDiagnostics;
    return safe(()=>d&&d.getPlaybackState?d.getPlaybackState():{playing:!!(window.__evPlaybackState&&window.__evPlaybackState.playing),time:NaN},{playing:false,time:NaN});
  }
  function monitor(){
    const now=performance.now(),state=playbackState();
    if(state.playing){
      if(stats.lastRaf!==null){
        const gap=now-stats.lastRaf;
        if(gap>34)stats.gap34++;
        if(gap>100)stats.gap100++;
        if(gap>stats.maxRafGap)stats.maxRafGap=gap;
      }
      stats.lastRaf=now;
      const d=window.__ethvelleAudioSchedulerDiagnostics;
      const ctx=safe(()=>d&&d.getContext&&d.getContext(),null);
      if(ctx&&ctx.state==='running'&&Number.isFinite(ctx.currentTime)){
        const offset=ctx.currentTime*1000-now;
        if(stats.clockOffset===null)stats.clockOffset=offset;
        else stats.maxClockDrift=Math.max(stats.maxClockDrift,Math.abs(offset-stats.clockOffset));
      }
    }else{
      stats.lastRaf=null;stats.clockOffset=null;
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(monitor);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(monitor);

  function buildReport(){
    const now=performance.now(),elapsed=Math.max(.001,(now-(diag.startedAt||now))/1000);
    const d=window.__ethvelleAudioSchedulerDiagnostics;
    const state=playbackState();
    const ctx=safe(()=>d&&d.getContext&&d.getContext(),null);
    const pool=safe(()=>d&&d.getPoolState?d.getPoolState():null,null)||{};
    const budget=window.__evDecoderBudget||{};
    const stop=diag.stopCounts||{};
    const times=(diag.sourceNodeTimes||[]).filter(t=>now-t<=10000);
    const lastSecond=times.filter(t=>now-t<=1000).length;
    const rateWindowSec=Math.max(1,Math.min(10,times.length?((now-Math.max(now-10000,times[0]))/1000):1));
    const rate10=times.length/rateWindowSec;
    let playbackStatsText='unavailable';
    if(ctx){
      const ps=safe(()=>ctx.playbackStats,null);
      if(ps!==null&&ps!==undefined){
        const get=(...keys)=>{for(const k of keys){const v=safe(()=>ps[k],undefined);if(v!==undefined&&v!==null)return v;}return 'n/a';};
        const count=get('underrunCount','totalUnderrunCount','underruns','underrun_count');
        const duration=get('underrunDuration','totalUnderrunDuration','underrunTime','underrun_time');
        playbackStatsText='underrun count='+count+', duration='+duration+'; raw='+trunc(safe(()=>JSON.stringify(ps),String(ps)),500);
      }
    }
    const videos=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('video'));
    const playingVideos=videos.filter(v=>!v.paused&&!v.ended).length;
    const sourceRows=[];
    const srcs=safe(()=>d&&d.getProjectAudioSources?d.getProjectAudioSources():[],[]);
    const grouped=new Map();
    srcs.forEach(src=>{
      const key=String(src.src||'');
      if(!grouped.has(key))grouped.set(key,{ids:[],types:[],src:key});
      const row=grouped.get(key);row.ids.push(src.id);row.types.push(src.type);
    });
    grouped.forEach(row=>{
      const entry=safe(()=>d&&d.cache&&d.cache.get(row.src),null);
      const status=entry&&entry.status?entry.status:'not requested';
      const error=entry&&entry.error?'  -  '+trunc(entry.error,140):'';
      sourceRows.push('  clips=['+row.ids.join(',')+'] types='+Array.from(new Set(row.types)).join(',')+' status='+status+error);
    });
    let lines=[];
    lines.push('ETHVELLE PLAYBACK DIAGNOSTICS v203');
    lines.push('Captured: '+new Date().toISOString());
    lines.push('Debug URL flag: evdebug=1; monitoring age: '+fmt(elapsed,1)+' s');
    lines.push('');
    lines.push('[Scheduler]');
    lines.push('stopScheduledAll total='+Number(stop.total||0)+'; drift='+Number(stop.drift||0)+', pause='+Number(stop.pause||0)+', start='+Number(stop.start||0)+', edit='+Number(stop.edit||0)+', other='+Number(stop.other||0));
    lines.push('AudioBufferSource nodes: '+lastSecond+' in last 1 s; '+fmt(rate10,2)+' nodes/s over the last '+fmt(rateWindowSec,1)+' s (max 10 s)');
    lines.push('Anchor re-creations: '+Number(diag.anchorRecreations||0));
    lines.push('');
    lines.push('[AudioContext]');
    lines.push('state='+(ctx?ctx.state:'unavailable')+'; baseLatency='+(ctx?fmt(ctx.baseLatency,6)+' s':'n/a')+'; outputLatency='+(ctx?fmt(ctx.outputLatency,6)+' s':'n/a'));
    lines.push('currentTime='+(ctx?fmt(ctx.currentTime,6)+' s':'n/a')+'; max |ctx/performance clock drift|='+fmt(stats.maxClockDrift,3)+' ms');
    lines.push('playbackStats: '+playbackStatsText);
    lines.push('');
    lines.push('[Main thread]');
    lines.push('rAF gaps >34 ms: '+stats.gap34+'; >100 ms: '+stats.gap100+'; max gap: '+fmt(stats.maxRafGap,1)+' ms');
    lines.push('PerformanceObserver longtask: '+(observer?'available':'unavailable')+'; count='+stats.longTaskCount+'; total='+fmt(stats.longTaskMs,1)+' ms');
    lines.push('');
    lines.push('[Video]');
    lines.push('DOM video elements: '+videos.length+'; currently playing: '+playingVideos);
    lines.push('decoder budget pool='+Number(budget.pool||3)+' / warm='+Number(budget.warm||2)+'; pool state size='+Number(pool.size||0)+' (free='+Number(pool.free||0)+', busy='+Number(pool.busy||0)+')');
    lines.push('rebuffer count='+Number(diag.rebufferCount||0)+'; kick count='+Number(diag.kickCount||0)+'; __evRebuffer='+String(window.__evRebuffer));
    lines.push('');
    lines.push('[Audio decode source status]');
    lines.push(sourceRows.length?sourceRows.join('\n'):'  no video/audio sources found in project');
    return lines.join('\n');
  }
  async function copyReport(){
    const report=buildReport();
    try{
      if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){await navigator.clipboard.writeText(report);button.textContent='Playback report copied';}
      else throw new Error('Clipboard API unavailable');
    }catch(_e){
      try{
        const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=report;ta.setAttribute('readonly','');ta.style.position='fixed';ta.style.opacity='0';document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();
        const ok=document.execCommand('copy');ta.remove();
        button.textContent=ok?'Playback report copied':'Copy failed';
      }catch(_err){button.textContent='Copy failed';}
    }
    button.disabled=true;setTimeout(()=>{button.disabled=false;button.textContent='Copy playback report';},1800);
  }
  button.addEventListener('click',copyReport);
})();
</script>

<style id="ethvelle-v207-audio-saved-and-add-css">
  /* Saved bookmark: a fully solid white shape (no hollow centre) once the sound is saved. */
  #toolPanel .ethvelle-audio-action.favorite.is-saved{color:#FFFFFF!important;}
  #toolPanel .ethvelle-audio-action.favorite.is-saved svg{
    fill:#FFFFFF!important;stroke:#FFFFFF!important;stroke-width:1.7!important;stroke-linejoin:round!important;
  }
  /* Add-to-timeline switch: same control as the "Add animation" switch on the Character
     Animation blocks (.anim-toggle), with the same #0068FF "on" colour. */
  #toolPanel .ethvelle-audio-add{
    position:relative!important;justify-self:center!important;align-self:center!important;
    margin:0!important;padding:0!important;-webkit-appearance:none;appearance:none;
  }
  #toolPanel .ethvelle-audio-add::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:-12px -6px;}
  #toolPanel .ethvelle-audio-add.on,

  }

<script id="ethvelle-v206-audio-library-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  let activeTab='trending',searchQuery='',preview=null,previewSource='',favorites=new Set();
  const favKey='ethvelleAudioFavoritesV206';
  try{favorites=new Set(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(favKey)||'[]').map(String));}catch(_e){}
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const esc=value=>String(value==null?'':value).replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
  const assets=()=>{try{return (typeof getEditorAssets==='function'?getEditorAssets():[]).filter(a=>a&&editorAssetCategory(a)==='audio'&&String(a.src||'').trim());}catch(_e){return [];}};
  const fmt=value=>{const n=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(value)||0));return '00:'+String(n%60).padStart(2,'0');};
  const searchIcon='<svg class="editor-asset-search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"></circle><path d="m16 16 4.2 4.2"></path></svg>';
  const playIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 5.5v13l10-6.5z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/></svg>';
  const pauseIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 5v14M16 5v14" stroke-width="2.8"/></svg>';
  const bookmarkIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 4.8A1.8 1.8 0 0 1 7.8 3h8.4A1.8 1.8 0 0 1 18 4.8V21l-6-4-6 4z"/></svg>';
  const downloadIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 4v11M7.5 10.5 12 15l4.5-4.5M5 19.5h14"/></svg>';
  const tabDefs=[['trending','Trending'],['sfx','Sound FX'],['voice','Voice'],['saved','Saved'],['music','Music Clip'],['funny','Funny'],['swooshes','Swooshes'],['asmr','ASMR']];
  function tagsFor(item){return [item.name,item.description,item.keywords,item.tags,item.category,item.type].flat().map(v=>String(v||'')).join(' ').toLowerCase();}
  function matchesTab(item){
    const t=tagsFor(item);
    if(item&&item.audioSection&&activeTab!=='trending'&&activeTab!=='saved')return String(item.audioSection)===activeTab;
    if(activeTab==='trending')return true;
    if(activeTab==='saved')return favorites.has(String(item.id));
    if(activeTab==='music')return /music|song|bgm|soundtrack|track|melody|instrumental/.test(t);
    if(activeTab==='funny')return /funny|comedy|meme|cartoon|pop|boing|comic/.test(t);
    if(activeTab==='swooshes')return /swoosh|whoosh|swish|transition|sweep|whip/.test(t);
    if(activeTab==='asmr')return /asmr|whisper|tap|click|texture|ambient|soft|close.?up/.test(t);
    if(activeTab==='voice')return /voice|vocal/.test(t);
    if(activeTab==='sfx')return /sound effect|sfx|whoosh|swoosh|metallic|spooky|horror|flash|impact|zap|pop/.test(t);
    return true;
  }
  function currentItems(){
    const list=assets();
    const q=searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    return list.filter(item=>{
      if(!matchesTab(item))return false;
      if(!q)return true;
      return tagsFor(item).includes(q);
    });
  }
  /* ---------- Plan access (same rule as the animation clips) ----------
     An admin ticks the plans allowed to use a clip (asset.accessPlans). The CHEAPEST ticked plan
     is the floor: visitors on a cheaper plan see a lock and can neither preview, download nor add it. */
  function accessPlans(item){
    const plans=(typeof getPlans==='function'?getPlans():[])||[];
    const free=plans.find(p=>Number(p.price)===0)||{id:'p_free',name:'Free',price:0};
    const raw=item&&item.accessPlans;
    const ids=Array.isArray(raw)?raw.map(String).filter(Boolean):(raw==null||raw==='')?[]:[String(raw)];
    if(!ids.length)return [free];
    const res=ids.map(id=>{const n=id.toLowerCase();return plans.find(p=>String(p.id)===id)||plans.find(p=>String(p.name||'').toLowerCase()===n)||((n==='free'||n==='p_free')?free:null);}).filter(Boolean);
    return res.length?res:[free];
  }
  function requiredPlan(item){return accessPlans(item).slice().sort((a,b)=>Number(a.price||0)-Number(b.price||0))[0];}
  function isLocked(item){
    const req=requiredPlan(item);if(!req||Number(req.price)<=0)return false;
    const cur=typeof getMyPlanData==='function'?getMyPlanData():{id:'p_free',name:'Free',price:0};
    if(accessPlans(item).some(p=>String(p.id)===String(cur.id)))return false;
    return Number(cur.price||0)<Number(req.price||0);
  }
  function upgrade(item){
    const req=requiredPlan(item);
    try{if(typeof closePanel==='function')closePanel();}catch(_e){}
    if(typeof goTo==='function')goTo('payment');
    try{
      const p=(typeof getPlans==='function'?getPlans():[]).find(x=>req&&String(x.id)===String(req.id));
      if(p){const card=document.querySelector('#screen-payment .plan-card[data-plan="'+p.id+'"]');if(card&&typeof selectPlan==='function')selectPlan(card);}
    }catch(_e){}
    if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Upgrade to '+((req&&req.name)||'a paid plan')+' to use this audio.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2400});
  }
  window.evAudioIsLocked=isLocked;window.evAudioUpgrade=upgrade;

  /* ---------- On-device storage ----------
     Nothing is downloaded in the background. A clip is fetched only when the visitor taps play or
     download; it is then kept in this browser's IndexedDB so every later play / download is served
     from the device with no network use. */
  const DB_NAME='ethvelle-audio-device',STORE='files';
  const mem=new Map(),objUrls=new Map(),pending=new Map();
  let dbPromise=null;
  function openDb(){
    if(dbPromise)return dbPromise;
    dbPromise=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
      try{
        const req=indexedDB.open(DB_NAME,1);
        req.onupgradeneeded=()=>{req.result.createObjectStore(STORE);};
        req.onsuccess=()=>resolve(req.result);req.onerror=()=>reject(req.error);
      }catch(e){reject(e);}
    });
    return dbPromise;
  }
  function idbGet(key){return openDb().then(db=>new Promise((res,rej)=>{const q=db.transaction(STORE,'readonly').objectStore(STORE).get(key);q.onsuccess=()=>res(q.result||null);q.onerror=()=>rej(q.error);})).catch(()=>null);}
  function idbPut(key,val){return openDb().then(db=>new Promise((res,rej)=>{const tx=db.transaction(STORE,'readwrite');tx.objectStore(STORE).put(val,key);tx.oncomplete=()=>res(true);tx.onerror=()=>rej(tx.error);tx.onabort=()=>rej(tx.error);})).catch(()=>false);}
  const keyOf=item=>String(item&&item.src||'');
  async function localBlob(item){
    const key=keyOf(item);if(!key)return null;
    if(mem.has(key))return mem.get(key);
    const rec=await idbGet(key);
    if(rec&&rec.blob){mem.set(key,rec.blob);return rec.blob;}
    return null;
  }
  const typeByExt={mp3:'audio/mpeg',m4a:'audio/mp4',aac:'audio/aac',wav:'audio/wav',ogg:'audio/ogg',oga:'audio/ogg',opus:'audio/ogg',flac:'audio/flac',webm:'audio/webm'};
  function guessType(src,given){
    const g=String(given||'').split(';')[0].trim().toLowerCase();
    if(/^audio\//.test(g))return g;
    const m=String(src).split(/[?#]/)[0].match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,5})$/i);
    return (m&&typeByExt[m[1].toLowerCase()])||'audio/mpeg';
  }
  async function fetchBlob(src,onProgress){
    const res=await fetch(src,{mode:'cors',credentials:'omit'});
    if(!res.ok)throw new Error('HTTP '+res.status);
    const total=Number(res.headers.get('content-length'))||0,type=guessType(src,res.headers.get('content-type'));
    if(!res.body||!res.body.getReader){const b=await res.blob();onProgress(1);return new Blob([b],{type});}
    const reader=res.body.getReader(),chunks=[];let got=0;
    for(;;){const r=await reader.read();if(r.done)break;chunks.push(r.value);got+=r.value.length;onProgress(total?Math.min(.98,got/total):-1);}
    onProgress(1);return new Blob(chunks,{type});
  }
  // Returns the clip as a Blob from the device, downloading it (with the popup) the first time only.
  function ensureBlob(item){
    const key=keyOf(item);
    if(pending.has(key))return pending.get(key);
    const p=(async()=>{
      const have=await localBlob(item);if(have)return have;
      openPop(item.name||'Audio');
      try{
        const blob=await fetchBlob(key,setPop);
        await idbPut(key,{blob,type:blob.type,savedAt:Date.now()});
        mem.set(key,blob);
        try{if(navigator.storage&&navigator.storage.persist)navigator.storage.persist();}catch(_e){}
        await donePop();
        return blob;
      }catch(err){closePop();throw err;}
    })().finally(()=>pending.delete(key));
    pending.set(key,p);return p;
  }
  function urlFor(item,blob){
    const key=keyOf(item);if(objUrls.has(key))return objUrls.get(key);
    const u=URL.createObjectURL(blob);objUrls.set(key,u);return u;
  }

  /* ---------- Download popup (with animation) ---------- */
  let popEl=null,popHide=0;
  function ensurePop(){
    if(popEl&&popEl.isConnected)return popEl;
    popEl=document.createElement('div');popEl.id='evAudioDl';popEl.setAttribute('role','status');popEl.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
    popEl.innerHTML='<div class="evad-card"><div class="evad-ring"><svg class="evad-arrow" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 4v11M7.5 10.5 12 15l4.5-4.5"/></svg><svg class="evad-check" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12.5l4.5 4.5L19 7.5"/></svg><i class="evad-tray"></i></div><div class="evad-title">Downloading audio…</div><div class="evad-name"></div><div class="evad-bar"><b></b></div><div class="evad-pct">0%</div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(popEl);return popEl;
  }
  function openPop(name){
    clearTimeout(popHide);const el=ensurePop();
    el.classList.remove('done','indet');el.querySelector('.evad-title').textContent='Downloading audio…';
    el.querySelector('.evad-name').textContent=String(name||'');el.querySelector('.evad-bar b').style.width='0%';el.querySelector('.evad-pct').textContent='0%';
    void el.offsetWidth;el.classList.add('show');
  }
  function setPop(frac){
    const el=popEl;if(!el)return;
    if(frac<0){el.classList.add('indet');el.querySelector('.evad-pct').textContent='';return;}
    el.classList.remove('indet');const pct=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(frac*100)));
    el.querySelector('.evad-bar b').style.width=pct+'%';el.querySelector('.evad-pct').textContent=pct+'%';
  }
  function donePop(){
    const el=popEl;if(!el)return Promise.resolve();
    el.classList.remove('indet');el.classList.add('done');el.querySelector('.evad-title').textContent='Saved to your device';
    el.querySelector('.evad-bar b').style.width='100%';el.querySelector('.evad-pct').textContent='100%';
    return new Promise(resolve=>{popHide=setTimeout(()=>{el.classList.remove('show');resolve();},850);});
  }
  function closePop(){clearTimeout(popHide);if(popEl)popEl.classList.remove('show');}

  /* ---------- Preview / download / add ---------- */
  let previewToken=0;
  function stopPreview(){
    previewToken++;
    if(preview){try{preview.pause();preview.currentTime=0;}catch(_e){};preview=null;previewSource='';}
    document.querySelectorAll('#ethvelleAudioLibraryResults .ethvelle-audio-preview.is-playing').forEach(btn=>{
      btn.classList.remove('is-playing');btn.innerHTML=playIcon;btn.setAttribute('aria-label','Preview sound');
    });
  }
  async function previewItem(item,button){
    if(isLocked(item)){upgrade(item);return;}
    const src=String(item.src||'');if(!src)return;
    if(preview&&previewSource===src&&!preview.paused){stopPreview();return;}
    stopPreview();
    const token=previewToken;
    let url=src;
    try{url=urlFor(item,await ensureBlob(item));}
    catch(_e){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not save this audio to your device. Playing it online.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2200});}
    if(token!==previewToken)return;            // another tap happened while it was downloading
    try{
      preview=new Audio();previewSource=src;preview.preload='auto';preview.volume=1;preview.muted=false;preview.src=url;
      preview.setAttribute('playsinline','');
      if(button&&button.isConnected){button.classList.add('is-playing');button.innerHTML=pauseIcon;button.setAttribute('aria-label','Pause preview');}
      preview.addEventListener('ended',stopPreview,{once:true});
      preview.addEventListener('error',()=>{stopPreview();if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not preview this audio file',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800});},{once:true});
      const p=preview.play();if(p&&typeof p.catch==='function')p.catch(()=>{stopPreview();if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Tap the play button again to start the preview.',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:2200});});
    }catch(_e){stopPreview();}
    markDeviceState();
  }
  const audioExt={'audio/mpeg':'mp3','audio/mp3':'mp3','audio/wav':'wav','audio/x-wav':'wav','audio/wave':'wav','audio/ogg':'ogg','audio/webm':'webm','audio/mp4':'m4a','audio/x-m4a':'m4a','audio/aac':'aac','audio/flac':'flac'};
  function downloadName(item,src,blobType){
    const base=String(item.name||'audio').replace(/\s*\(\d+(\.\d+)?s\)/g,'').replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|\u0000-\u001f]+/g,'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,'-')||'audio';
    let ext=audioExt[String(blobType||'').split(';')[0].toLowerCase()];
    if(!ext){const m=String(src).split(/[?#]/)[0].match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,5})$/i);ext=m?m[1].toLowerCase():'mp3';}
    return /\.[a-z0-9]{2,5}$/i.test(base)?base:base+'.'+ext;
  }
  function saveBlob(blob,filename){
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');
    a.href=url;a.download=filename;a.rel='noopener';a.style.display='none';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();
    setTimeout(()=>{a.remove();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);},4000);
  }
  // Runs only when the visitor taps the download icon. First time: popup + save on device; later: instant.
  async function downloadItem(item,btn){
    if(isLocked(item)){upgrade(item);return;}
    const src=String(item.src||'');if(!src)return;
    if(btn)btn.classList.add('is-busy');
    try{
      const blob=await ensureBlob(item);
      saveBlob(blob,downloadName(item,src,blob.type));
      if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Download started',{icon:toastIcons.check,duration:1400});
      markDeviceState();
    }catch(_e){
      // Host without CORS: the page cannot read the bytes, so let the browser fetch the file itself.
      try{const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=src;a.download=downloadName(item,src,'');a.target='_blank';a.rel='noopener';a.style.display='none';document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();setTimeout(()=>a.remove(),1000);}
      catch(_e2){if(typeof showToast==='function')showToast('Could not download this audio file',{icon:toastIcons.info,duration:1800});}
    }finally{if(btn)btn.classList.remove('is-busy');}
  }
  function saveFavorites(){try{localStorage.setItem(favKey,JSON.stringify(Array.from(favorites)));}catch(_e){}}
  function addItem(item){
    if(isLocked(item)){upgrade(item);return;}
    if(typeof addAdminAssetToProject==='function')addAdminAssetToProject(item.id);
  }
  // Marks rows whose clip is already stored on this device.
  async function markDeviceState(){
    const host=$('ethvelleAudioLibraryResults');if(!host)return;
    for(const row of host.querySelectorAll('.ethvelle-audio-row')){
      const item=assets().find(a=>String(a.id)===String(row.dataset.audioRow));if(!item)continue;
      const on=!!(await localBlob(item));
      row.classList.toggle('on-device',on);
      const meta=row.querySelector('.ethvelle-audio-meta');if(meta)meta.textContent=metaFor(item,on);
    }
  }
  function metaFor(item,onDevice){
    const req=requiredPlan(item),paid=req&&Number(req.price)>0;
    return 'Audio'+(paid?' · '+req.name:'')+(onDevice?' · On this device':'');
  }
  const lockIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="5" y="11" width="14" height="9" rx="2"/><path d="M8 11V8a4 4 0 0 1 8 0v3"/></svg>';
  function renderResults(){
    const host=$('ethvelleAudioLibraryResults');if(!host)return;
    const rows=currentItems();
    if(!rows.length){
      host.innerHTML=activeTab==='saved'
        ?(searchQuery.trim()
          ?'<div class="ethvelle-audio-empty"><strong>No saved sounds match</strong>Try another search term.</div>'
          :'<div class="ethvelle-audio-empty"><strong>No saved sounds yet</strong>Tap the bookmark on any sound to save it here.</div>')
        :'<div class="ethvelle-audio-empty"><strong>No audio clips here</strong>Try another category or search term. Audio added from the admin panel appears here automatically.</div>';
      return;
    }
    host.innerHTML='<div class="ethvelle-audio-library-list">'+rows.map(item=>{
      const id=esc(item.id),name=esc(item.name||'Audio'),duration=fmt(item.duration||item.sourceDuration||3);
      const saved=favorites.has(String(item.id)),locked=isLocked(item),req=requiredPlan(item);
      const lockBtn=locked?`<button type="button" class="anim-lock-btn ethvelle-audio-lock" data-ev-audio-action="upgrade" data-ev-audio-id="${id}" aria-label="Upgrade to ${esc(req.name||'a paid plan')} to use ${name}">🔒 ${esc(req.name||'Paid')}</button>`
        :`<button type="button" class="anim-toggle ethvelle-audio-add" role="switch" aria-checked="false" data-ev-audio-action="add" data-ev-audio-id="${id}" aria-label="Add ${name} to timeline" title="Add to timeline"></button>`;
      return `<article class="ethvelle-audio-row${locked?' is-locked':''}" data-audio-row="${id}">
        <button type="button" class="ethvelle-audio-preview" data-ev-audio-action="preview" data-ev-audio-id="${id}" aria-label="${locked?'Locked  -  upgrade to preview':'Preview sound'}">${locked?lockIcon:playIcon}</button>
        <div class="ethvelle-audio-info"><span class="ethvelle-audio-name" title="${name}">${name}</span><span class="ethvelle-audio-meta">${esc(metaFor(item,false))}</span></div>
        <span class="ethvelle-audio-duration">${duration}</span>
        <button type="button" class="ethvelle-audio-action download" data-ev-audio-action="download" data-ev-audio-id="${id}" aria-label="${locked?'Locked  -  upgrade to download':'Download '+name}" title="${locked?'Upgrade to download':'Download'}">${downloadIcon}</button>
        <button type="button" class="ethvelle-audio-action favorite ${saved?'is-saved':''}" data-ev-audio-action="favorite" data-ev-audio-id="${id}" aria-label="${saved?'Remove saved sound':'Save sound'}" title="${saved?'Saved':'Save'}">${bookmarkIcon}</button>
        ${lockBtn}
      </article>`;
    }).join('')+'</div>';
    markDeviceState();
  }
  function renderTabs(){
    const host=$('ethvelleAudioTabs');if(!host)return;
    host.innerHTML=tabDefs.map(([id,label])=>`<button type="button" class="ethvelle-audio-tab ${activeTab===id?'active':''}" role="tab" aria-selected="${activeTab===id?'true':'false'}" data-audio-tab="${id}">${label}</button>`).join('');
    host.querySelectorAll('[data-audio-tab]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      activeTab=btn.dataset.audioTab||'trending';renderTabs();renderResults();
      const strip=$('ethvelleAudioTabs');const active=strip&&strip.querySelector('.active');if(active)active.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',inline:'nearest'});
    }));
  }
  window.filterEthvelleAudioLibrary=function(value){
    searchQuery=String(value||'');
    const wrap=$('ethvelleAudioSearchWrap');if(wrap)wrap.classList.toggle('has-query',!!searchQuery);
    renderResults();
  };
  window.clearEthvelleAudioLibrarySearch=function(){const input=$('ethvelleAudioLibrarySearch');if(input){input.value='';window.filterEthvelleAudioLibrary('');try{input.focus({preventScroll:true});}catch(_e){input.focus();}}};
  window.renderEthvelleAudioLibraryResults=renderResults;
  window.renderEthvelleAudioLibrary=function(){
    const body=$('panelBody');if(!body)return;
    body.innerHTML=`
      <div class="editor-asset-toolbar">
        <div class="editor-asset-search-wrap" id="ethvelleAudioSearchWrap">
          ${searchIcon}
          <input class="editor-asset-search" id="ethvelleAudioLibrarySearch" type="search" aria-label="Search audio files" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Search sounds and effects…" oninput="filterEthvelleAudioLibrary(this.value)">
          <button type="button" class="editor-asset-search-clear" id="ethvelleAudioSearchClear" onclick="clearEthvelleAudioLibrarySearch()" aria-label="Clear search">×</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="ethvelle-audio-tabs" id="ethvelleAudioTabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Audio categories"></div>
      <div id="ethvelleAudioLibraryResults" class="editor-asset-results"></div>`;
    renderTabs();renderResults();
    const host=$('ethvelleAudioLibraryResults');
    if(host&&!host.dataset.evV206Actions){
      host.dataset.evV206Actions='1';
      host.addEventListener('click',e=>{
        const btn=e.target&&e.target.closest?e.target.closest('[data-ev-audio-action]'):null;if(!btn)return;
        const id=String(btn.dataset.evAudioId||'');
        const item=assets().find(a=>String(a.id)===id);if(!item)return;
        const action=btn.dataset.evAudioAction;
        if(action==='preview'){previewItem(item,btn);return;}
        if(action==='download'){downloadItem(item,btn);return;}
        if(action==='upgrade'){upgrade(item);return;}
        if(action==='favorite'){
          if(favorites.has(id))favorites.delete(id);else favorites.add(id);
          saveFavorites();
          const nowSaved=favorites.has(id);
          btn.classList.toggle('is-saved',nowSaved);
          btn.setAttribute('aria-label',nowSaved?'Remove saved sound':'Save sound');
          btn.setAttribute('title',nowSaved?'Saved':'Save');
          // Inside the Saved filter an un-saved sound leaves the list straight away.
          if(activeTab==='saved')renderResults();
          return;
        }
        if(action==='add'){
          // Same behaviour as the animation switch: it flips on while the sound is added.
          btn.classList.add('on');btn.setAttribute('aria-checked','true');
          stopPreview();addItem(item);
          setTimeout(()=>{if(btn.isConnected){btn.classList.remove('on');btn.setAttribute('aria-checked','false');}},700);
        }
      });
    }
  };
  window.openEthvelleAudioLibrary=function(){
    stopPreview();activeTab='trending';searchQuery='';
    const panel=$('toolPanel'),backdrop=$('panelBackdrop');if(!panel||!backdrop)return;
    const title=$('panelTitle'),body=$('panelBody');if(title)title.textContent='Audio Library';
    if(body){body.innerHTML='';window.renderEthvelleAudioLibrary();}
    panel.classList.add('open');backdrop.classList.add('active');
  };
